Verdict: INCOMPLETE
  - required component "rag" cannot be executed yet: Requires an authorized evaluation organisation and programme, populated Vault corpus with embedded chunks, verified and version-pinned official answer sources with SHA-256, reviewed gold items with source quotes and resolved open checks, and enough representative items. The RAG regression runner now resolves reviewed source keys within tenant/program scope and fails closed on incomplete assessments, but it cannot attest the provider-resolved generator and judge versions and cannot produce usable PQ evidence. run-pq still has no integrated RAG phase. These are source, review, attribution and PQ-integration blockers as well as operational ingestion/provider prerequisites; enabling ingestion alone cannot qualify a model.
